\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\mathbb{E}\{\tr T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-2}S_{i}^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\tr T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}ST_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\tr T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}S_{z}^{-1}ST_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}=\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu).

Thus,
B^{2}=\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{\mu}}\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)
and with probability
1-\eta
we can bound (I.3) with

\left\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{S}-S)^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\right\|\leq 2\ln(2/\eta)\left(\frac{4\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1/2}}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{n\mu^{1/2}}\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)}\right):=\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta).

Now combining this with (I.1) and (I.2), with probability
1-3\eta
,

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{S}-S)^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\|\leq\frac{\kappa\delta_{z}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta).

For
(II)
we use the resolvent identity, Lemma
F.5
, and
F.2
. With probability
1-\eta,

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}_{z}-S_{z})(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}.

Lastly, for we use that
\text{(III)}\quad\left\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}-S)\right\|\leq\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|\|(\hat{S}-S)\|\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}.

This holds on the event from Lemma
E.3
and using Lemma
B.3
with

A^{*}=S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{1/2}.
Combining the three bounds with a union for the distinct high probability events involved, we have with probability
1-4\eta
,

\left\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{T}_{\mu}-T_{\mu})\right\|_{op}\leq\delta=\frac{\kappa\delta_{z}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}+\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta).\qed

Lemma F.11

(
\Delta_{1}
-bound)
.

Suppose the following inequalities hold

\displaystyle\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}_{z}-S_{z})\|=\delta_{z}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2},

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\gamma}_{2},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|S_{z,i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}.

Then, conditional on data, with probability
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{14\Big\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}+\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{4\mu\lambda}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}\Big\}}.

If additionally
\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{T}_{\mu}-T_{\mu})\|_{op}\leq\delta_{B}<1/2
. Then, with probability
1-\eta
,

\|\Delta_{1}\|\leq 2\delta_{B}\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{14\Big\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}+\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{4\mu\lambda}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}\Big\}},

where
\Delta_{h}=\|h_{0}-\hat{h}\|.

Proof.

Let
u=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\hat{w}_{ij}h_{ij}.
Conditional on the data,
\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u
is Gaussian. Using Lemma
B.10
, with probability
1-\eta
,

\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}}.

Note that
\mathbb{E}_{h}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{ij}\|^{2}
. Within each term, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{ij}\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{\hat{w}_{i}-\hat{w}_{j}\}\|^{2}\leq\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}\|^{2}+\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{j}\|^{2}.

We can rewrite the following quantities

\displaystyle\phi(Z_{i})\hat{\epsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\phi(Z_{i})(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i}))=\phi(Z_{i})(h_{0}(X_{i})-\hat{h}(X_{i})+\epsilon_{i})=S_{i}(h_{0}-\hat{h})+\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}[\hat{\epsilon}\phi(Z)]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{n}[(Y-\hat{h}(X))\phi(Z)]=\hat{S}(h_{0}-\hat{h})+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)].

Using this,
\hat{w}_{i}
becomes

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{i}

\displaystyle=\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(S_{i}(h_{0}-\hat{h})+\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i})

\displaystyle+S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}(h_{0}-\hat{h})+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)])

\displaystyle-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}(h_{0}-\hat{h})+\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]).

Combining the terms gives

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{i}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\left\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{i}+S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\}(h_{0}-\hat{h})}_{A}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}}_{B}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left\{S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\right\}\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]}_{C}.

We can use the parallelogram law to write

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{ij}\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}\|^{2}.

Now, focus on the individual components of this expression and note that by Lemma
B.3

\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|_{op}\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}.

(A)

We add and subtract the estimated
\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{T}_{\mu}
, resulting in

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{i}+S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\}(h_{0}-\hat{h})\pm 2\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\}\Delta_{h}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(S_{i}-\hat{S})\|\Delta_{h}+\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(S_{i}-\hat{S})^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\|\Delta_{h}+\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\|\Delta_{h}

\displaystyle+\|2\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}\right\}\|\Delta_{h}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|S_{i}-\hat{S}\|\Delta_{h}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}\|S_{i}-\hat{S}\|\Delta_{h}+\frac{\kappa_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\Delta_{h}+2\Delta_{h}.

(B)

Next,

\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\mu\lambda}}\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|.

(C)

Finally,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\right\}\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]\|+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]\|

Recall we have split
\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}
into seven terms. Combining them,

\displaystyle 2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 14\Big\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4\mu\lambda}E_{n}\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]\|^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}E_{n}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}+\frac{\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\epsilon\phi(Z)]\|^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}E_{n}\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}\Big\}.

Now with the events from Lemma
E.3
,

F.6
,
F.8
, and
F.8
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{w}_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 14\Big\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}+\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{4\mu\lambda}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}\Big\}.

Furthermore, by Lemma
F.4
, if
\delta_{B}<1/2
, then with probability
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{1}\|

\displaystyle=\|(\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}-T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1})u\|=\|(T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}-\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1})u\|\leq 2\delta_{B}\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{B}\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{14\Big\{\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}+\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{4\mu\lambda}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}\Big\}}.

∎

Lemma F.12

(
\Delta_{2}
-bound)
.

Let,
\Delta_{2}=T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})h_{ij}\right\}
. Suppose the following bounds hold:

\displaystyle\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(\hat{S}_{z}-S_{z})\|=\delta_{z}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\|(\hat{S}_{z}-S_{z})(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{z}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{T}_{\mu}-T_{\mu})\|\leq\delta_{B}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\|S-\hat{S}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\gamma}_{1}

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}_{n}[\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}]\|

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\gamma}_{2}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\gamma^{\prime}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|S_{z,i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\big\|S_{i}^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\big\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{n}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1/2}S_{z,i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}.

Then, conditional on data, with probability
1-\eta
,

\|\Delta_{2}\|\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\bar{\Delta}_{2}},

where

\displaystyle\bar{\Delta}_{2}

\displaystyle={30}(1+\delta_{B})\Bigg\{\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}(\kappa^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{1}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{4}\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}\Bigg\}

and
\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}=\|h_{\mu,\lambda}-\hat{h}\|
according to Lemma
F.9
.

Proof.

Let
u=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})h_{ij}.
Conditional on the data, with probability
1-\eta
,

\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}}.

Furthermore,
\mathbb{E}_{h}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})\|^{2}.
Note that

\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij}=\frac{\hat{w}_{i}-w_{i}}{\sqrt{2}}-\frac{\hat{w}_{j}-w_{j}}{\sqrt{2}}.

Considering each
\hat{w}_{i}-w_{i}
we can write

\displaystyle\hat{w}_{i}-w_{i}

\displaystyle=S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i}))+S_{i}^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})

\displaystyle-\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\hat{\epsilon}_{i}+S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{n}[\hat{\varepsilon}\phi(Z)]-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{n}[\hat{\varepsilon}\phi(Z)]\}

\displaystyle=S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i}))+S_{i}^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})

\displaystyle-\{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i}))+S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{n}[(Y-\hat{h}(X))\phi(Z)]

\displaystyle\quad-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{n}[(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X))\phi(Z)]\}.

Within each term of
\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})\|^{2}
,

\displaystyle\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{2}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{(\hat{w}_{i}-w_{i})-(\hat{w}_{j}-w_{j})\}\|^{2}\leq\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}+\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{j}\|^{2}.

Using the parallelogram law again,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{h}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}u\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}\leq\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|(T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}-\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1})\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}+\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1+\delta_{B})\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}.

Now, we decompose
\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}
further:

\displaystyle\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}=\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\underbrace{\Big[S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)}_{A}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\big(\hat{h}(X_{i})-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)}_{B}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{S_{i}^{*}\Big[(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)}_{C}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big(S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)-E_{n}[\big(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i})\big)\phi(Z_{i})]\Big)}_{D}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\Big[S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)}_{E}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}\Big[(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)}_{F}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big(S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)-\mathbb{E}_{n}[\big(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i})\big)\phi(Z_{i})]\Big)\Bigg\}}_{G}.

(A)

By Lemma
F.13
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Big[S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|

\displaystyle+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|.

Generally, note that

\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})=S_{i}(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})+\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}
. For the first term,

\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\left\{\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{i}\|b_{\mu,\lambda}+\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|\right\}.

For the second term,

\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|\leq\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\{\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|\}.

Together, this yields

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Big[S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]\phi(Z_{i})\big(Y_{i}-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\left\{\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{i}\|b_{\mu,\lambda}+\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|\right\}+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\{\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\|\phi(Z_{i})\epsilon_{i}\|\}.

(B)

Next,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\phi(Z_{i})\big(\hat{h}(X_{i})-h_{\mu,\lambda}(X_{i})\big)\|=\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{i}(\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda})\big)\|\leq\frac{\kappa}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}.

(C)

Next,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S_{i}^{*}\Big[(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\|

\displaystyle=\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(S_{z}-\hat{S}_{z})(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S_{z}}+\mu)^{-1}\|\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}.

(D)

Next,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big(S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)-E_{n}[\big(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i})\big)\phi(Z_{i})]\Big)\|

\displaystyle=\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big\{(S-\hat{S})\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)-\hat{S}(h_{\mu,\lambda}-\hat{h})-\mathbb{E}_{n}(\epsilon\phi(Z))\Big\}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{\lambda}\|S_{i}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|.

(E)

By Lemma
F.13
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Big[S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\ \|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})\|+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|S_{z,i}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}\|v_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1/2}S_{z,i}\|v_{\mu,\lambda}.

(F)

Next,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}\Big[(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big]S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\|\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}.

(G)

Finally,

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\Big(S\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)-\mathbb{E}_{n}[\big(Y_{i}-\hat{h}(X_{i})\big)\phi(Z_{i})]\Big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\left\{\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}(S-\hat{S})\big(h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\|+\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\hat{S}(h_{\mu,\lambda}-\hat{h})\|+\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{n}(\epsilon\phi(Z))\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{2\sqrt{\mu\lambda}}\|S_{z,i}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\|.

We combine these bounds for
\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1+\delta_{B})\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}
. Note that we split
\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}
into seven terms (A-G). However, some of them were split into more terms. Overall, we obtained
15
terms, meaning we can bound the norm
\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}
by the sum of the individual norms times
15
(i.e.
\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}\leq 15\sum\|.\|^{2}.

As such,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(1+\delta_{B})\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\Delta w_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq{30}(1+\delta_{B})\Bigg\{\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}(\kappa^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{1}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{4}\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}\Bigg\}:=\bar{\Delta}_{2}.

Therefore, conditional on the data and the assumed high probability events, with probability
1-\eta
,

\|\Delta_{2}\|\leq\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\bar{\Delta}_{2}}.

∎

Lemma F.13

(Inverse estimation error bound)
.

If
\delta_{z}\leq 1/2
and
\|\hat{S}-S\|_{op}\leq\tilde{\gamma}_{1}
then, for any
u\in\mathcal{H}_{z}
,

\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\}u\|\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}u\|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\delta_{z}\|u\|.

Proof.

Using the resolvent identity, we can write

\displaystyle\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\}u\|

\displaystyle=\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\pm\hat{S}^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\}u\|

\displaystyle=\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\{(S-\hat{S})^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-\hat{S}^{*}\{(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}-(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\}u\}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}u\|+\|\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\hat{S}^{*}(\hat{S}_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\sum_{r=1}^{\infty}\{(\hat{S}_{z}-S_{z})(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}\}^{r}u\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\|(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}u\|+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}2\delta_{z}\|u\|.

∎

F.4
Main result

Lemma F.14

(Decomposition of the bootstrap error)
.

\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}=\Delta_{1}+\Delta_{2},

where
\Delta_{1}=(\hat{T}_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}-T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1})(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\hat{w}_{ij}h_{ij})
and
\Delta_{2}=T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\hat{w}_{ij}-w_{ij})h_{ij}\right\}.

Proof.

The proof follows by matching symbols with Lemma G.3 in
34
.
∎

Theorem F.1

(Feasible bootstrap)
.

Suppose
n\geq\max\{N_{\delta_{z}},3N_{\delta}\}
such that
\delta_{B}=3\delta\leq 1/2
and
\delta_{z}\leq 1/2
. Then with probability
1-15\eta
,

\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|\leq C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\lambda}A+\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}B+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}C+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}D\Bigg],

where

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{1}(b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+2\sqrt{\Delta_{h}\lambda\mu}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\delta_{z}(\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\kappa_{z}^{2}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}\Big).

Proof.

Let
C_{\eta}=\left\{1+\sqrt{2\log(1/\eta)}\right\}

We first collect the events and then determine the probability. Using
Lemma

F.11
and
Lemma

F.12
, with probability
1-2\eta
,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\Delta_{1}\|+\|\Delta_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta_{B}C_{\eta}\sqrt{14\Big(\frac{\kappa^{2}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{4\kappa^{4}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{2}\kappa_{x}\Delta_{h}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}+4\Delta_{h}+\frac{\gamma^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}{\lambda^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}{4\lambda\mu}\Big)}

\displaystyle\qquad{}+C_{\eta}\Big[30(1+\delta_{B})\Big\{\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}(\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}(\kappa^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\gamma^{\prime})+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{1}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\delta_{z}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}(\delta_{z}^{\prime})^{2}v_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}b_{\mu,\lambda}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}+\frac{\kappa_{z}^{4}\kappa^{2}}{4\lambda\mu^{2}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}^{2}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}^{2}}{4\lambda\mu}\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}\Big\}\Big]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\eta}\Bigg[2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Bigg(\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\Delta_{h}+\frac{2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}\Delta_{h}+\frac{\kappa_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\Delta_{h}+\frac{2\sqrt{\Delta_{h}\lambda\mu}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}+\frac{\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Bigg(\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}\big(b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}\big)+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\big(\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}\big)+\frac{\kappa}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{\lambda}\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{\kappa}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}}+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\frac{\kappa\kappa_{z}^{2}}{2\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{2}}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Bigg)\Bigg]

\displaystyle=C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\lambda}A+\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}B+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}C+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}D\Bigg].

Here,

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{1}(b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+2\sqrt{\Delta_{h}\lambda\mu}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\delta_{z}(\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big),

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\kappa_{z}^{2}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}\Big).

Now, the events with bounds
\delta_{\mu}^{\prime},\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime},\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime},\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime},\tilde{\gamma}_{1},\tilde{\gamma}_{2},\tilde{\gamma}
each hold with probability
1-\eta
. Moreover,
\delta_{z},\delta_{z}^{\prime}
also hold with probability
1-\eta
. Notice that
\delta_{B}
depends only on
\delta
(Lemma
F.10
) which holds with
1-2\eta
since we have already accounted for
\tilde{\gamma}_{1}
and
\delta_{z}
.
When using the consistency bounds from
Lemma

F.9
, the bound holds with probability
1-13\eta
when taking into account the
2\eta
from before. For
\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}
, we need to consider the unconsidered events from our Bahadur representation.
Note
\|\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\|\leq\|\mathbb{E}_{n}(U_{i})\|+\Delta_{U}

with

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}_{n}(U_{i})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\delta_{z}^{\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\delta_{z}^{\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}.

Here, only
\delta_{z}^{\prime\prime}
is new.
In
\Delta_{U}
(see Theorem
E.1
) we observe that only
\tilde{\gamma}_{3}
(through
\gamma
) is unaccounted for.
Thus, we can use the derived bound for
\Delta_{U}
with an additional probability of
1-\eta
and

\Delta_{U}\leq 2\delta\gamma+\gamma_{1}+\xi_{1}+\left[2\delta^{2}+\frac{\delta_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\{\delta_{z}(\kappa+\tilde{\gamma}_{1})+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{2}\}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\lambda\mu}\{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\delta_{z}(1+2\delta_{z})(\kappa+\tilde{\gamma}_{1})\}\right]\|h_{0}-h_{\mu,\lambda}\|.

Given that
\Delta_{h}
’s probabilistic part is only,
\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}
we can bound the overall
\|\mathfrak{B}-Z\|
with the above events and probability
1-15\eta
.
∎

Appendix G
Gaussian coupling

G.1
Polynomial decay

Assumption G.1

(Spectrum of
S_{x}
)
.

S_{x}
is an operator with eigenvalues
\nu_{s}(S_{x})
arranged in nonincreasing order. The spectrum of
S_{z}
decays polynomially, i.e.

\nu_{s}(S_{x})\asymp\omega_{x}s^{-1/(\rho_{x}-1)},

with
\rho_{x}\in[1,2].

Assumption G.2

(Spectrum of
S_{z}
)
.

S_{z}
is a positive, self-adjoint operator with eigenvalues
\nu_{s}(S_{Z})
arranged in nonincreasing order. The spectrum of
S_{z}
decays polynomially, i.e.

\nu_{s}(S_{z})\asymp\omega_{z}s^{-1/(\rho_{z}-1)},

with
\rho_{z}\in[1,2].

Lemma G.1

(Effective dimension bound)
.

Suppose Assumptions
G.1
and
G.2
hold. Then, the effective dimensions are bounded by

\displaystyle\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\rho_{z},\omega_{z}}\mu^{-\rho_{x}},

\displaystyle\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\lambda^{-\rho_{x}},

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\lambda^{-\rho_{x}},

with
\rho_{x},\rho_{z}\in[1,2].

Proof.

The bound for
\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)
follows directly from Proposition K.2 of
34
by taking
\rho_{z}=1+1/\beta
, where this
\beta
represents the notation used in their paper.
For
\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)=\tr\{(T_{\mu}+\lambda)^{-2}T_{\mu}\}
note that
T_{\mu}\preceq T\preceq S_{x}
.
Following
34
, define a generalized effective dimension of
S_{x}
as

\psi(m,c)=\sum_{s=m+1}^{\infty}\frac{\nu_{s}}{(\nu_{s}+\lambda)^{c}}.

Here,

\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\tr\{(T_{\mu}+\lambda)^{-1}T_{\mu}\}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\tr\{(T+\lambda)^{-1}T\}\leq\frac{1}{\lambda}\tr\{(S_{x}+\lambda)^{-1}S_{x}\}=\frac{1}{\lambda}\psi(0,1)\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\frac{1}{\lambda}\frac{1}{\lambda^{\rho_{x}-1}}=\lambda^{-\rho_{x}}.

The last inequality follows again by applying their Proposition K.2.
Lastly,

\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)=\tr T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}S_{z}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}ST_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}\leq\tr T_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}S^{*}(S_{z}+\mu)^{-1}ST_{\mu,\lambda}^{-1}=\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\lambda^{-\rho_{x}}.

∎

G.2
Main result

Remark G.1

(Rate of
\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}
)
.

We analyze the rate of
\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}
. For this, note that when considering
\mu\leq\lambda
,

\displaystyle\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\delta_{z}^{\prime\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\delta_{z}^{\prime}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{U})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{U})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\lambda}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{U})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{U}).

Theorem G.1

(Gaussian approximation)
.

Suppose
n
satisfies the rate Assumption
D.2
i.e.
n\geq\max\{N_{\delta_{z}},N_{\delta}\}
,
\|U_{i}\|\leq a\lesssim\frac{\widetilde{M}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}
and that Assumptions
G.1
and
G.2
hold. In addition, suppose the assumptions for the bias upper bound hold (
Lemma

C.2
). Then, there exits a sequence
(Z_{i})_{1\leq i\leq n}
of Gaussians in
\mathcal{H}_{x}
, with covariance
\Sigma
such that with probability
1-\eta
,

\|\sqrt{n}(\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\|\lesssim Q_{\bullet}(T,n,\lambda,\mu)\widetilde{M}\log(36/\eta)+Q_{\mathrm{res}},

where

Q_{\bullet}=\inf_{m\geq 1}\left\{\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}+\frac{m^{2}\log(m^{2})}{\sqrt{n\mu\lambda}}\right\}

and

Q_{\mathrm{res}}=\left(\frac{log(12/\eta)^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda\mu}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{log(12/\eta)^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{log(12/\eta)^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda\mu}\right).

Proof.

Recall that by Theorem
E.1
, with probability
1-5\eta
,

\|\sqrt{n}(\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}\|\lesssim B(n,\mu,\lambda,\eta,h_{0})+V(n,\mu,\lambda,\eta).

Now, we study the behavior
V
and
B
by plugging in the tractable behavior of the effective dimensions, while noting that
\mu\leq\lambda
by the bias argument. Let
l(\eta)=\ln(2/\eta)
and recall that

\displaystyle\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta)

\displaystyle=2l(\eta)\left(\frac{4\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1/2}}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\kappa\kappa_{x}^{1/2}}{n\mu^{1/2}\lambda^{\rho_{x}}}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(l(\eta)\sqrt{\frac{1}{n\mu^{1/2}\lambda^{\rho_{x}}}}\right),

\displaystyle V(n,\mu,\lambda,\eta)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{1/2+(3/2)\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{1+(1/2)\rho_{x}}\mu^{1/2+\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+(1/2)\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda^{1/2+(1/2)\rho_{x}}\mu^{3/4}}\right),

\displaystyle B(n,\mu,\lambda,\eta,h_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}\vee\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta)^{2}\right)b_{\mu,\lambda}=\mathcal{O}(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}b_{\mu,\lambda}).

Now, we proceed to simplify using
\mu\leq\lambda
:

\displaystyle B+V

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda^{2}\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}b_{\mu,\lambda}\vee\frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{1/2+(3/2)\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{1+(1/2)\rho_{x}}\mu^{1/2+\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+(1/2)\rho_{z}}}\ \vee\ \frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda^{1/2+(1/2)\rho_{x}}\mu^{3/4}}\right))

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}b_{\mu,\lambda}\vee\frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}\vee\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\right).

We arrive at the final form for polynomial decay:

\|\sqrt{n}(\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}\|\lesssim\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)^{3}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\right).

In general form,

\|\sqrt{n}(\hat{h}-h_{\mu,\lambda})-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}U_{i}\|\lesssim\left(\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda\mu}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda\mu}\right).

Recall the upper bound for the
\|U_{i}\|\lesssim\frac{\widetilde{M}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}

where
\widetilde{M}
encompasses all constants in the upper bound; see Corollary
D.1
for details. Now, using Theorem A.1 from
34
together with Lemma
D.3
, with probability
1-\eta,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(U_{i}-Z_{i})\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\inf_{m\geq 1}\left\{\sqrt{\log(6/\eta)}\sigma(\Sigma,m)+\frac{am^{2}\log(m^{2}/\eta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\inf_{m\geq 1}\left\{\sqrt{\log(6/\eta)}\frac{\sqrt{8}\bar{\sigma}}{\lambda}\sigma(T,m)+\frac{am^{2}\log(m^{2}/\eta)}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\inf_{m\geq 1}\left\{\sqrt{\log(6/\eta)}\frac{\widetilde{M}}{\lambda}\sigma(T,m)+\frac{\widetilde{M}m^{2}\log(m^{2}/\eta)}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}\right\}.

Using a union bound, the Gaussian approximation holds with
1-6\eta
. Rescaling
\eta/6

gives the result.∎

Appendix H
Bootstrap coupling

Assumption H.1

(Combined rate condition)
.

Assume a universal
n
that satisfies all rate conditions imposed with

n\geq\max\{3N_{\delta},N_{\delta_{z}},\mu^{-2}\lambda^{-1}\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda\mu)^{-1}\}.

Remark H.1

(Dominant terms in
\delta
)
.

We study the dominant terms in
\delta
from
Lemma

F.10
. Note that this is a
different

\delta
than the one we used for the Gaussian coupling:

\displaystyle\delta

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\delta_{z}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}^{2}}{\lambda\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{z}}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{1}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}+\tilde{\delta}(\mu,\lambda,\eta)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\mu\lambda}\vee\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\lambda\mu^{2}}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\vee\frac{1}{n\sqrt{\lambda}\mu^{3/2}}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\mathfrak{m}(\lambda,\mu)}{n\mu^{1/2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\vee\frac{1}{n\sqrt{\lambda}\mu^{3/2}}\vee\sqrt{\frac{1}{n\mu^{1/2}\lambda^{\rho_{x}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\vee\frac{1}{n\sqrt{\lambda}\mu^{3/2}}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\mathcal{O}\left(1\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\mu}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\right).

All terms converge to zero because of the rate condition, taking
\lambda=\mu^{\iota}
. This rate also holds for
\delta_{B}
.

Lemma H.1

(Feasible bootstrap rate)
.

Suppose
n
satisfies the combined rate condition
H.1
and that Assumptions
G.1
and
G.2
hold. In addition, suppose that the assumptions for the bias upper bound hold (
Lemma

C.2
). Then, with probability
1-15\eta
,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{l(\eta)}{\mu^{2}\sqrt{n}\lambda}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}\Bigg].

Proof.

Recall the elements of the feasible bootstrap

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{1}(b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\delta_{B}\Big(\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\Big)+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}+\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\delta_{B}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}(n^{-1/2})\vee\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}\left\{\delta_{B}n^{-1/2}\vee\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}v_{\mu,\lambda}\vee\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}n^{-1/2}),

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(2\kappa^{2}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{1}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime}}\Big))

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\delta_{B}(b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}})+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}),

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\kappa\Delta_{h}+\tfrac{1}{2}\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+2\sqrt{\Delta_{h}\lambda\mu}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\delta_{z}(\kappa b_{\mu,\lambda}+\sqrt{\gamma^{\prime}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{1}b_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tfrac{1}{2}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big))

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\Bigg((\delta_{B}\Big(\Delta_{h}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big)+\Big(\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}+\delta_{z}^{\prime}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\Bigg(\delta_{B}\Big(b_{\mu,\lambda}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big)+\Big(\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}\Big)\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}(\delta_{B}\tilde{\gamma}_{2}+\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2})+\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}(\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2})+\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}),

\displaystyle D

\displaystyle=2\delta_{B}\sqrt{14}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa_{z}\sqrt{\kappa_{x}}\Delta_{h}\Big)+\sqrt{30(1+\delta_{B})}\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\kappa\kappa_{z}^{2}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}).

We can now study the error bound of the bootstrap in detail:

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|\leq C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{1}{\lambda}A+\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}B+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}C+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}D\Bigg].

Since
\mu\leq\lambda
,
c_{det}=\frac{1}{\lambda\sqrt{\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}n^{-1/2}+c_{det}\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\big(c_{det}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\big)\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\big(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}(\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2})+\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\lambda}\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime\prime}}n^{-1/2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\Delta_{h_{\mu,\lambda}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\big(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}(\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}+n^{-1/2})+\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}))+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\big(\sqrt{\delta_{\mu}^{\prime}}(\delta_{z}v_{\mu,\lambda}+n^{-1/2})+\delta_{z}b_{\mu,\lambda}\big)\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}))\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\delta_{B}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}\sqrt{\frac{\mu\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}))\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\lambda^{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu^{\rho_{z}}}}\lambda^{\alpha}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}))\Big]

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Big[\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda}\mu}\lambda^{\alpha}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}\mu}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}))\Big].

For
Q_{\mathrm{res}}
we already derived

Q_{\mathrm{res}}\lesssim\left(\frac{1}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}\lambda^{\alpha}+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}+\frac{1}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\right).

Plugging this in gives, for polynomial decay,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{\lambda^{\alpha-1}}{\mu^{2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{1}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\lambda^{\alpha}}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2+\rho_{z}}}+\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}+\frac{1}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\Bigg].

In general form,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{\eta}\Bigg[\frac{l(\eta)}{\mu^{2}\sqrt{n}\lambda}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{l(\eta)^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{l(\eta)^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}\Bigg],

which holds with probability
1-15\eta.

∎

Theorem H.1

(Bootstrap approximation)
.

Suppose
n
satisfies the combined rate condition of
H.1
, and that Assumptions
G.1
and
G.2
hold. In addition, suppose that the assumptions for the bias upper bound hold (
Lemma

C.2
). Then, there exists
a random variable
Z
whose conditional distribution given U is Gaussian with covariance
\Sigma
, such that with probability
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathfrak{B}-Z^{\prime}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}\lesssim\widetilde{M}\log(6/\eta)^{3/2}R_{\bullet}(n,\lambda,\mu)+R_{\mathrm{res}}|U\right]\geq 1-\eta,

where

\displaystyle R_{\bullet}

\displaystyle=\inf_{m\geq 1}\left[m^{1/4}\left\{\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)}{\mu\lambda n}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\mu^{2}\lambda^{2}}\right\}^{1/4}+\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}\right],

\displaystyle R_{\mathrm{res}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2l\left(\frac{2\eta}{15}\right)}\Bigg[\left\{\frac{l(\frac{\eta}{15})}{\mu^{2}n^{1/2}\lambda}+\frac{l(\frac{\eta}{15})^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}\right\}\|h_{\mu,\lambda}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}+\frac{l(\frac{\eta}{15})}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}n^{1/2}}+\frac{l(\frac{\eta}{15})^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{l(\frac{\eta}{15})^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}\Bigg].

Proof.

Let,
l(\eta)=\log(2/\eta)
and
\|h_{\mu,\lambda}-h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}:=b_{\mu,\lambda}
, Recall from Lemma
H.1
, with probability
1-\eta
,

\displaystyle\|\mathfrak{B}-Z_{\mathfrak{B}}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}\lesssim\sqrt{2\log(15/\eta)}\Bigg[\frac{\log(30/\eta)}{\mu^{2}\sqrt{n}\lambda}b_{\mu,\lambda}+\frac{\log(30/\eta)}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\log(30/\eta)^{2}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}b_{\mu,\lambda}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\log(30/\eta)^{3}\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\log(30/\eta)^{2}\sqrt{\mathfrak{n}_{z}(\mu)}}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2}}\Bigg].

This defines
R_{\mathrm{res}}.

Now, apply Corollary B.1 from
34
taking
W=Z_{\mathfrak{B}}
and
W^{\prime}={\mathfrak{B}}
. Then, there must exist
Z^{\prime}
with the desired conditional distribution, such that with probability
1-\eta
, conditional upon
\sigma(U)
,

\displaystyle\|Z^{\prime}-\mathfrak{B}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C^{\prime}\log(6/\eta)^{3/2}\inf_{m\geq 1}\left[m^{1/4}\left\{\frac{a^{2}\sigma^{2}(\Sigma,0)}{n}+\frac{a^{4}}{n^{2}}\right\}^{1/4}+\sigma(\Sigma,m)\right]+R_{\mathrm{res}}.

Similar for the Gaussian coupling we use that
a\leq\frac{\widetilde{M}}{\sqrt{\lambda\mu}}
, due to Corollary
D.1
and Lemma
D.3
:

\displaystyle\|Z-\mathfrak{B}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim C^{\prime}\log(6/\eta)^{3/2}\inf_{m\geq 1}\left[m^{1/4}\left\{\frac{a^{2}\sigma^{2}(\Sigma,0)}{n}+\frac{a^{4}}{n^{2}}\right\}^{1/4}+\sigma(\Sigma,m)\right]+R_{\mathrm{res}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\widetilde{M}\log(6/\eta)^{3/2}\inf_{m\geq 1}\left[m^{1/4}\left\{\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)}{\mu\lambda n}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\mu^{2}\lambda^{2}}\right\}^{1/4}+\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}\right]+R_{\mathrm{res}}.\qquad\qed

Appendix I
Uniform confidence band

I.1
High level summary

1.

Q(n,\lambda,\mu,\eta)=Q_{\bullet}(T,n,\lambda,\mu)\widetilde{M}\log(12/\eta)+Q_{\mathrm{res}}
;

2.

R(n,\lambda,\mu,\eta)=\widetilde{M}\log(6/\eta)^{3/2}R_{\bullet}(n,\lambda,\mu)+R_{\mathrm{res}}
;

3.

L(\lambda,\mu,\eta)=\sqrt{\frac{1}{4}\underline{\sigma}^{2}\mathfrak{\tilde{m}}(\lambda,\mu)}-\left\{2+\sqrt{2\ln(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\frac{2\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{\lambda}}
;

4.

B(\lambda,\mu)=\sqrt{n}\frac{C_{\alpha}\lambda^{\alpha}\|T^{-\alpha}h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}}{1-C_{\beta}r\sqrt{\mu/\lambda}}
.

We now explicitly provide upper bounds for
(Q,R,L,B)
under polynomial decay.

B and L:

By the bias upper bound

B(\lambda,\mu)=\sqrt{n}\frac{C_{\alpha}\lambda^{\alpha}\|T^{-\alpha}h_{0}\|_{\mathcal{H}_{x}}}{1-C_{\beta}r\sqrt{\mu/\lambda}}.

Lemma
D.2
implies that

\left\lVert Z\right\rVert\gtrsim L(\lambda,\mu,\eta)=\sqrt{\frac{1}{4}\underline{\sigma}^{2}\mathfrak{\tilde{m}}(\lambda,\mu)}-\left\{2+\sqrt{2\ln(1/\eta)}\right\}\sqrt{\frac{2\bar{\sigma}^{2}}{\lambda}}.

Q:
Note that
Q=Q_{\bullet}+Q_{\mathrm{res}}
. The latter is already derived in Theorem
G.1
. For
Q_{\bullet}
, following Proposition K.1 of
34
,

\sigma(T,m)\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega}m^{1/2-1/(2\rho_{x}-2)}.

Recall that

Q_{\bullet}=\inf_{m\geq 1}\left\{\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}+\frac{m^{2}\log(m^{2})}{\sqrt{n\mu\lambda}}\right\}.

Equating main terms and solving for
m
,

\displaystyle\frac{m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{2}}{\sqrt{n\lambda\mu}}

\displaystyle m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}-2}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\lambda}{n\mu}\Big)^{\!1/2}

\displaystyle m^{-\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\lambda}{n\mu}\Big)^{\!1/2}

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{n\mu}{\lambda}\Big)^{\frac{\rho_{x}-1}{3\rho_{x}-2}}.

Finally, we can derive an upper bound for
Q_{\bullet}
by plugging this
m
in

Q_{\bullet}\lesssim\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega}\frac{m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}}{\lambda}=\frac{1}{\lambda}\Big(\frac{n\mu}{\lambda}\Big)^{\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(3\rho_{x}-2)}}.

R:

R=R_{\bullet}+R_{\mathrm{res}}
. The latter is derived in Theorem
H.1
. For
R_{\bullet}
, recall that

\displaystyle R_{\bullet}(n,\lambda,\mu)

\displaystyle=\inf_{m\geq 1}\left[m^{1/4}\left\{\frac{\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)}{\mu\lambda n}+\frac{1}{n^{2}\mu^{2}\lambda^{2}}\right\}^{1/4}+\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}\right].

Now in order for,
\tilde{\mathfrak{m}}(\lambda,\mu)/({n\lambda\mu})>\frac{1}{n^{2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{2}}

we need
n>({\mathfrak{\tilde{m}}(\lambda,\mu)\mu\lambda})^{-1}
. This directly follows from the combined rate
H.1
when taking
\lambda=\mu
. Equating the main terms and solving for
m
yields

\displaystyle m^{1/4}\Big(\frac{\lambda^{-\rho_{x}}}{n\lambda\mu}\Big)^{1/4}

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle m^{1/4}\left(\frac{\lambda^{-\rho_{x}}}{n\lambda\mu}\right)^{1/4}

\displaystyle=\frac{m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}}{\lambda}

\displaystyle m^{-\frac{1}{4}+\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}

\displaystyle=(n\mu)^{1/4}\lambda^{(\rho_{x}-3)/4}

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle=\Big((n\mu)^{1/4}\lambda^{(\rho_{x}-3)/4}\Big)^{-\frac{4(\rho_{x}-1)}{\rho_{x}-3}}

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle=(n\mu)^{-\frac{\rho_{x}-1}{\rho_{x}-3}}\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}-1)}.

Plugging this in gives us the upper bound for
R_{\bullet}
:

R_{\bullet}\lesssim\frac{\sigma(T,m)}{\lambda}\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\frac{m^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2\rho_{x}-2}}}{\lambda}\lesssim_{\rho_{x},\omega_{x}}\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}(n\mu)^{-\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(\rho_{x}-3)}}.

I.2
Restrictions for valid inference

We derive restrictions on possible data-generating processes that allow us to use the guarantees of
34
.

I.2.1
B\ll L

We require

n^{1/2}\frac{\lambda^{\alpha}}{1-\sqrt{\mu/\lambda}}\ll\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}.

Rearranging yields

n^{1/2}\ll(1-\sqrt{\mu/\lambda})\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}/2+\alpha)}\quad\Longrightarrow\quad n\ll(1-\sqrt{\mu/\lambda})^{2}\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\lesssim\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}.

I.2.2
Q+R\ll L

We now determine the sample size
n
required to ensure that the approximation errors
Q_{\bullet}
and
R_{\bullet}
are dominated by the variance
L=\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}
.

Condition for
Q_{\bullet}
:

Using the
Q_{\bullet}
derivation from before, the condition
Q_{\bullet}\ll L
requires

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda}\left(\frac{n\mu}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(3\rho_{x}-2)}}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}

\displaystyle\left(\frac{n\mu}{\lambda}\right)^{\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(3\rho_{x}-2)}}

\displaystyle\ll\lambda^{1-\rho_{x}/2}

\displaystyle\frac{n\mu}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\gg\left(\lambda^{\frac{2-\rho_{x}}{2}}\right)^{\frac{2(3\rho_{x}-2)}{\rho_{x}-2}}

\displaystyle n

\displaystyle\gg\mu^{-1}\lambda^{-(3\rho_{x}-3)}.

Condition for
R_{\bullet}
:

Using the
R_{\bullet}
derivation from before, the condition
R_{\bullet}\ll L
requires that

\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}(n\mu)^{-\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(\rho_{x}-3)}}\ll\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}.

Canceling the
\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}
term, this simplifies to

(n\mu)^{-\frac{\rho_{x}-2}{2(\rho_{x}-3)}}\ll 1\iff n\gg\mu^{-1}.

Conditions for
Q_{\mathrm{res}}
:

Using the definition of
Q_{\mathrm{res}}
from Theorem
G.1
and plugging in the bias and effective dimension upper bound, we can derive

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{\alpha-1}}{n\mu^{1+\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\Longleftrightarrow n\geq\lambda^{\alpha-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\iff n\geq\lambda^{-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{3}}\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\rho_{z}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n\lambda\mu^{1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\Longleftrightarrow n\geq\lambda^{-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\mu^{-(1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z})}.

Conditions for
R_{\mathrm{res}}
:

Using the definition of
R_{\mathrm{res}}
from Theorem
G.1
and plugging in the bias and effective dimension upper bound, we can derive

(1)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{\alpha-1}}{\mu^{2}\sqrt{n}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad n\geq\mu^{-4}\lambda^{2\alpha-2+\rho_{x}}

(2)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda\mu^{3/2}\sqrt{n}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad n\geq\mu^{-3}\lambda^{-2+\rho_{x}}

(3)

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda^{\alpha-3/2}}{n\mu^{2+\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad n\geq\mu^{-(2+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{\alpha-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}

(4)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n^{3/2}\lambda^{2}\mu^{\frac{3}{2}+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad n\geq\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{-\frac{4}{3}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{3}}

(5)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n\lambda^{3/2}\mu^{2+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}}}\leq\lambda^{-\rho_{x}/2}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad n\geq\mu^{-(2+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z})}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}.

Combining restrictions gives the conditions summarized by Table
2
.

Table 2
:
Final restrictions on
\lambda,\mu
.

Component

Sufficient restriction on
\lambda,\mu

B\ll L

n\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}

Q_{\bullet}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-1}\lambda^{-(3\rho_{x}-3)}

R_{\bullet}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-1}

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(1)}\ll L

n\gg\lambda^{\alpha-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(2)}\ll L

n\gg\lambda^{-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{3}}\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}-\rho_{z}}

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(3)}\ll L

n\gg\lambda^{-1+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\mu^{-\bigl(1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}\bigr)}

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(1)}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-4}\lambda^{2\alpha-2+\rho_{x}}

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(2)}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-3}\lambda^{-2+\rho_{x}}

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(3)}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-(2+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{\alpha-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(4)}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{-\frac{4}{3}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{3}}

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(5)}\ll L

n\gg\mu^{-\bigl(2+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}\bigr)}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}

I.2.3
Parameter restrictions from combined bounds

We now derive the conditions on parameters
\alpha,\rho_{x},\rho_{z},
and
\iota
required to ensure that the set of data generating processes defined by
Q+R\ll L
(lower bound) and
B\ll L
(upper bound) is non-empty. Recall the upper bound is
n\lesssim\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}
. We substitute
\lambda=\mu^{\iota}
and analyze the regime
\mu\to 0
. For
Q_{\bullet}
, the condition
\mu^{-1}\lambda^{-(3\rho_{x}-3)}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}
becomes

\mu^{-\bigl(1+\iota(2\rho_{x}-3-2\alpha)\bigr)}\ll 1\iff 1+\iota(2\rho_{x}-3-2\alpha)<0\iff 2\rho_{x}<3+2\alpha-\frac{1}{\iota}.

For
R_{\bullet}
, the condition
\mu^{-1}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}
implies
\mu^{-1}\ll\mu^{-\iota(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}
. This holds for
\mu\to 0
if and only if

1<\iota(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)\iff\rho_{x}+2\alpha>\frac{1}{\iota}.

Next, we analyze the restrictions imposed by
Q_{\mathrm{res}}
. We require the lower bound exponent to be strictly larger than the upper bound exponent (in terms of
\mu^{-k}
decay):

(1)

\displaystyle\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\gtrsim\lambda^{\alpha-1+\rho_{x}/2}\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}\implies\rho_{z}+1<\iota\left(3\alpha+\frac{3}{2}\rho_{x}-1\right)

(2)

\displaystyle\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\gtrsim\lambda^{-1+\rho_{x}/3}\mu^{-\left(\frac{1}{3}+\rho_{z}\right)}\implies\rho_{z}+\frac{1}{3}<\iota\left(2\alpha+\frac{4}{3}\rho_{x}-1\right)

(3)

\displaystyle\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\gtrsim\lambda^{-1+\rho_{x}/2}\mu^{-\left(1+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}\right)}\implies\rho_{z}+2<\iota\left(4\alpha+3\rho_{x}-2\right).

Similarly, the conditions from
R_{\mathrm{res}}
yield

(1)

\displaystyle\mu^{-4}\lambda^{2\alpha-2+\rho_{x}}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\iff\iota(4\alpha+2\rho_{x}-2)>4

(2)

\displaystyle\mu^{-3}\lambda^{-2+\rho_{x}}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\iff\iota(2\alpha+2\rho_{x}-2)>3

(3)

\displaystyle\mu^{-(2+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{\alpha-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\iff 2\rho_{z}+4<\iota(6\alpha+3\rho_{x}-3)

(4)

\displaystyle\mu^{-(1+\rho_{z})}\lambda^{-\frac{4}{3}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{3}}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\iff 3\rho_{z}+3<\iota(6\alpha+4\rho_{x}-4)

(5)

\displaystyle\mu^{-\left(2+\frac{1}{2}\rho_{z}\right)}\lambda^{-\frac{3}{2}+\frac{\rho_{x}}{2}}\ll\lambda^{-(\rho_{x}+2\alpha)}\iff\rho_{z}+4<\iota(4\alpha+3\rho_{x}-3).

These combined parameter restrictions are summarized in Table
3
.

Table 3
:
Parameter restrictions ensuring
Q+R\ll L
and
B\ll L
simultaneously

Component

Restriction

Q_{\bullet}

2\rho_{x}<3+2\alpha-\frac{1}{\iota}

R_{\bullet}

\rho_{x}+2\alpha>\frac{1}{\iota}

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(1)}

\rho_{z}+1<\iota\left(3\alpha+\tfrac{3}{2}\rho_{x}-1\right)

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(2)}

\rho_{z}+\tfrac{1}{3}<\iota\left(2\alpha+\tfrac{4}{3}\rho_{x}-1\right)

Q_{\mathrm{res}}^{(3)}

\rho_{z}+2<\iota\!\left(4\alpha+3\rho_{x}-2\right)

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(1)}

\iota\left(4\alpha+2\rho_{x}-2\right)>4

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(2)}

\iota\left(2\alpha+2\rho_{x}-2\right)>3

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(3)}

2\rho_{z}+4<\iota\!\left(6\alpha+3\rho_{x}-3\right)

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(4)}

3\rho_{z}+3<\iota\!\left(6\alpha+4\rho_{x}-4\right)

R_{\mathrm{res}}^{(5)}

\rho_{z}+4<\iota\!\left(4\alpha+3\rho_{x}-3\right)

I.3
Valid and sharp inference

Proof of Theorem
1
.

We appeal to Proposition 1 of
34
. To establish the claimed validity and sharpness, it suffices to show that there exist choices of
\eta
and
\delta
satisfying:

\frac{\Delta(n,\lambda,\mu,\eta)+B(\lambda,\mu)}{L(\lambda,\mu,1-\alpha-2\eta)-\Delta(n,\lambda,\mu,\eta)}\leq\delta\leq\frac{1}{2},

where
\Delta:=Q+R
represents the total approximation error. We select
\eta=1/n
and
\delta=1/\log(n)
. The parameter restrictions summarized in Tables
2
and
3
ensure that the bias
B
and approximation error
\Delta
decay strictly faster than the variance lower bound
L
. Specifically, our bounds imply

\frac{B}{L}=\mathcal{O}(n^{-\varepsilon_{1}})\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{\Delta}{L}=\mathcal{O}(n^{-\varepsilon_{2}})

for some
\varepsilon_{1},\varepsilon_{2}>0
. Consequently, for sufficiently large
n
, we have
\Delta\leq L/2
, allowing us to bound the left hand side

\frac{\Delta+B}{L-\Delta}\leq\frac{\Delta+B}{L/2}=2\left(\frac{\Delta}{L}+\frac{B}{L}\right)=\mathcal{O}(n^{-(\varepsilon_{1}\wedge\varepsilon_{2})}).

Since a polynomial decay dominates a logarithmic decay, we have
\mathcal{O}(n^{-\varepsilon})=o(1/\log(n))
. Therefore, for sufficiently large
n
,

\frac{\Delta+B}{L-\Delta}\leq\frac{1}{\log(n)}=\delta.

Finally, since
n
is large,
\delta=1/\log(n)\leq 1/2
. Thus, the condition on the incremental factor is satisfied. By Proposition 1 of
34
, this choice of
\delta
guarantees that the confidence sets are valid with tolerance
\tau=\mathcal{O}(\eta)=\mathcal{O}(1/n)
and satisfy sharpness with slack
2\delta=2/\log(n)
.
∎
